=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\llm\colab_chat.py ===

"""LangChain wrapper for the existing Colab /generate endpoint.
Place at: app/llm/colab_chat.py

The Colab server is unchanged: it takes {"messages": [...]} and streams plain text.
Tool calling is done through the prompt: the model is told to answer with one JSON
object {"tool": "...", "args": {...}} when it wants a tool, and plain text when done.
"""
import json
import time
import uuid
from typing import Any, List, Optional

import requests
from app.llm import usage
from app.llm.gate import llm_slot
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, BaseMessage, ToolMessage
from langchain_core.outputs import ChatGeneration, ChatResult
from langchain_core.utils.function_calling import convert_to_openai_tool
from pydantic import Field

TOOL_INSTRUCTIONS = """You can use tools. Available tools:
{tool_list}

RULES:
- To use a tool, reply with ONLY one JSON object and nothing else:
  {{"tool": "<tool_name>", "args": {{<arguments>}}}}
- Call at most ONE tool per reply. Wait for its result before continuing.
- Use only the tools and argument names listed above.
- Once resolve_incident or escalate_incident succeeds, DO NOT call any more tools.
- When you have finished, reply in plain text with no JSON."""

TERMINAL_TOOLS = {"resolve_incident", "escalate_incident"}


class ColabChat(BaseChatModel):
    url: str                                   # COLAB_API_URL (the /generate endpoint)
    timeout: int = 180
    tools_spec: List[dict] = Field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def _llm_type(self) -> str:
        return "colab-generate"

    # ---- LangChain calls this so create_agent can "bind" tools to the model ----
    def bind_tools(self, tools: list, **kwargs: Any) -> "ColabChat":
        specs = [convert_to_openai_tool(t)["function"] for t in tools]
        return self.model_copy(update={"tools_spec": specs})

    # ---- message conversion ----
    def _tool_prompt(self) -> str:
        lines = []
        for s in self.tools_spec:
            props = s.get("parameters", {}).get("properties", {})
            args = ", ".join(f"{k}: {v.get('type', 'any')}" for k, v in props.items())
            lines.append(f"- {s['name']}({args}): {s.get('description', '')}")
        return TOOL_INSTRUCTIONS.format(tool_list="\n".join(lines))

    def _to_colab(self, messages: List[BaseMessage]) -> list:
        out, system_parts = [], []
        for m in messages:
            if m.type == "system":
                system_parts.append(m.content)
            elif m.type == "human":
                out.append({"role": "user", "content": m.content})
            elif m.type == "ai":
                if m.tool_calls:
                    tc = m.tool_calls[0]
                    content = json.dumps({"tool": tc["name"], "args": tc["args"]})
                else:
                    content = m.content
                out.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
            elif isinstance(m, ToolMessage):
                if m.name in TERMINAL_TOOLS and '"error"' not in str(m.content):
                    follow_up = ("The incident is now closed or escalated. Do NOT call any more tools. "
                                 "Reply with a two-sentence plain-text summary and no JSON.")
                else:
                    follow_up = "Continue with the next step in the playbook. Call ONE tool or give the final answer."
                out.append({"role": "user",
                            "content": f"[TOOL RESULT from {m.name}]: {m.content}\n{follow_up}"})
        if self.tools_spec:
            system_parts.append(self._tool_prompt())
        if system_parts:
            out.insert(0, {"role": "system", "content": "\n\n".join(system_parts)})
        return out

    # ---- parse the model's text into a tool call or a normal answer ----
    def _parse(self, text: str) -> AIMessage:
        names = {s["name"] for s in self.tools_spec}
        decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
        i = text.find("{")
        while self.tools_spec and i != -1:
            try:
                obj, _ = decoder.raw_decode(text[i:])
                if isinstance(obj, dict) and obj.get("tool") in names:
                    args = obj.get("args") or {}
                    if isinstance(args, dict):
                        return AIMessage(content="", tool_calls=[{
                            "name": obj["tool"], "args": args,
                            "id": f"call_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}", "type": "tool_call"}])
            except ValueError:
                pass
            i = text.find("{", i + 1)
        return AIMessage(content=text.strip())

    def _generate(self, messages: List[BaseMessage], stop: Optional[List[str]] = None,
                  run_manager: Any = None, **kwargs: Any) -> ChatResult:
        msgs = self._to_colab(messages)
        run_id, kind = usage.agent_context()          # 'triage' or 'resume', set by _consume
        tokens_in = usage.count_messages(msgs)
        text = ""
        with llm_slot(kind) as waited:
            t0 = time.time()
            with requests.post(self.url, json={"messages": msgs},
                               stream=True, timeout=self.timeout) as r:
                r.raise_for_status()
                for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=512, decode_unicode=True):
                    if chunk:
                        text += chunk
            seconds = time.time() - t0
        usage.log_call(kind, run_id, tokens_in, usage.count_tokens(text), seconds, waited)
        return ChatResult(generations=[ChatGeneration(message=self._parse(text))])

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\llm\gate.py ===

"""One priority gate in front of the single Colab model (D-13).

Chat, agent steps and document ingestion all call the same /generate endpoint, which handles
one request at a time. Without a gate they queue inside Colab in arrival order. Here the lowest
priority number goes first, so an agent step never waits behind a chat message that arrived later.

Do not nest llm_slot() calls in one thread: the inner call would wait for the outer one forever.
"""
import contextlib
import heapq
import itertools
import os
import threading
import time

PRIORITY = {"resume": 0, "verify": 1, "triage": 2, "chat": 3, "ingest": 4}
CHAT_WAIT_SECONDS = int(os.getenv("LLM_CHAT_WAIT_SECONDS", "90"))
_DEFAULT = object()


class LLMBusy(Exception):
    """The model stayed busy for longer than the caller was willing to wait."""


class _Gate:
    def __init__(self, slots: int = 1):
        self._cv = threading.Condition()
        self._free = slots
        self._waiting: list = []          # heap of (priority, arrival number)
        self._seq = itertools.count()

    def acquire(self, priority: int, timeout) -> None:
        entry = (priority, next(self._seq))
        deadline = None if timeout is None else time.monotonic() + timeout
        with self._cv:
            heapq.heappush(self._waiting, entry)
            try:
                while not (self._free > 0 and self._waiting[0] == entry):
                    remaining = None if deadline is None else deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if remaining is not None and remaining <= 0:
                        raise LLMBusy("The AI model is busy")
                    self._cv.wait(remaining)
                self._free -= 1
            finally:
                self._waiting.remove(entry)
                heapq.heapify(self._waiting)
                self._cv.notify_all()

    def release(self) -> None:
        with self._cv:
            self._free += 1
            self._cv.notify_all()

    def depth(self) -> int:
        with self._cv:
            return len(self._waiting)


GATE = _Gate(1)


@contextlib.contextmanager
def llm_slot(kind: str = "chat", wait_timeout=_DEFAULT):
    """with llm_slot("triage") as waited: ...   (waited = seconds spent in the queue).
    Chat gives up after LLM_CHAT_WAIT_SECONDS and raises LLMBusy; every other kind waits."""
    if wait_timeout is _DEFAULT:
        wait_timeout = CHAT_WAIT_SECONDS if kind == "chat" else None
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    GATE.acquire(PRIORITY.get(kind, 3), wait_timeout)
    waited = time.monotonic() - t0
    try:
        yield waited
    finally:
        GATE.release()

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\llm\usage.py ===

"""Token accounting for every LLM call (P0).

The Colab endpoint streams plain text and returns no token counts, so tokens are counted here
with the model's own tokenizer (loaded once in a background thread; falls back to chars / 3.5
if it cannot be downloaded). Each call is logged and saved to the llm_calls table.
"""
import contextvars
import logging
import os
import threading

log = logging.getLogger("uvicorn.error")

_TOKENIZER_NAME = os.getenv("TOKENIZER_NAME", "Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct")
_tok = None
_tok_state = "pending"          # pending | ok | failed
_load_lock = threading.Lock()


def warm() -> str:
    """Loads the tokenizer once and returns 'ok' or 'failed'."""
    global _tok, _tok_state
    with _load_lock:
        if _tok_state != "pending":
            return _tok_state
        try:
            from transformers import AutoTokenizer
            _tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(_TOKENIZER_NAME)
            _tok_state = "ok"
        except Exception as e:
            _tok_state = "failed"
            log.warning("Tokenizer %s unavailable (%s). Estimating tokens as chars / 3.5.",
                        _TOKENIZER_NAME, str(e)[:120])
    return _tok_state


threading.Thread(target=warm, daemon=True, name="tokenizer-warmup").start()


def count_tokens(text: str) -> int:
    if not text:
        return 0
    if _tok is not None:
        try:
            return len(_tok.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return int(len(text) / 3.5) + 1


def count_messages(messages: list) -> int:
    """Prompt size for a list of {"role", "content"} dicts (about 4 tokens of framing per message)."""
    return sum(count_tokens(str(m.get("content", ""))) + 4 for m in messages) + 3


# ---- which agent run a model call belongs to ----
_run_ctx = contextvars.ContextVar("triage_run", default=None)
_active = {"run_id": None, "kind": "triage"}   # fallback: only one agent run is active at a time


def bind_run(run_id: str, kind: str = "triage") -> None:
    _run_ctx.set((run_id, kind))
    _active.update(run_id=run_id, kind=kind)


def unbind_run(run_id: str) -> None:
    _run_ctx.set(None)
    if _active["run_id"] == run_id:
        _active.update(run_id=None, kind="triage")


def agent_context() -> tuple:
    """(run_id, kind) for a model call made by the agent. kind is 'triage' or 'resume'."""
    ctx = _run_ctx.get()
    if ctx:
        return ctx
    return _active["run_id"], _active["kind"]


def log_call(kind: str, run_id, tokens_in: int, tokens_out: int, seconds: float, waited: float = 0.0) -> None:
    log.info("LLM %-7s run=%s in=%d out=%d gen=%.1fs wait=%.1fs",
             kind, run_id or "-", tokens_in, tokens_out, seconds, waited)
    try:
        from app import store
        store.add_llm_call(kind, run_id, tokens_in, tokens_out, seconds, waited)
    except Exception as e:
        log.warning("usage: could not save the LLM call: %s", str(e)[:150])

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\tools\aws.py ===

"""AWS lookups: find the EC2 instance a ticket is about and get its CURRENT public IP.
Public IPs change when an instance is stopped and started, so we ask AWS each time.
Needs only ec2:DescribeInstances."""
import os
import re
import time
from functools import cache

import boto3

STRICT = False  # the agent sets this True: no fallback to the static hosts in .env
ACTIVE = {}     # platform ("windows"/"linux") -> instance_id of the server the current ticket is about
_CACHE = {"at": 0.0, "instances": []}
_TTL = 30  # seconds


from botocore.config import Config

_CFG = Config(retries={"max_attempts": 3, "mode": "standard"}, connect_timeout=5, read_timeout=15)


@cache
def _ec2():
    return boto3.client("ec2", region_name=os.getenv("AWS_REGION"), config=_CFG)


def _flatten(inst: dict) -> dict:
    tags = {t["Key"]: t["Value"] for t in inst.get("Tags", [])}
    return {
        "instance_id": inst["InstanceId"],
        "name": tags.get("Name", ""),
        "state": inst["State"]["Name"],
        "platform": "windows" if inst.get("Platform") == "windows" else "linux",
        "public_ip": inst.get("PublicIpAddress", ""),
        "private_ip": inst.get("PrivateIpAddress", ""),
    }


def list_instances(force: bool = False) -> list:
    if force or time.time() - _CACHE["at"] > _TTL:
        pages = _ec2().get_paginator("describe_instances").paginate(
            Filters=[{"Name": "instance-state-name",
                      "Values": ["pending", "running", "stopping", "stopped"]}]
        )
        found = [_flatten(i) for p in pages for r in p["Reservations"] for i in r["Instances"]]
        _CACHE.update(at=time.time(), instances=found)
    return _CACHE["instances"]


def get_instance(instance_id: str) -> dict:
    """Fresh data for one instance (skips the cache, so a restarted instance shows its new IP)."""
    res = _ec2().describe_instances(InstanceIds=[instance_id])
    return _flatten(res["Reservations"][0]["Instances"][0])


def find_in_text(text: str):
    """The one instance whose Name tag, instance id or IP appears in the text. None if zero or several match."""
    low = text.lower()
    hits = []
    for inst in list_instances():
        for tok in (inst["name"], inst["instance_id"], inst["public_ip"], inst["private_ip"]):
            if tok and re.search(rf"(?<![\w.-]){re.escape(tok.lower())}(?![\w-])", low):
                hits.append(inst)
                break
    return hits[0] if len(hits) == 1 else None


def current_host(target: str):
    """Public IP of the active instance for this platform, or None if no instance was resolved."""
    iid = ACTIVE.get(target)
    if not iid:
        return None
    inst = get_instance(iid)
    label = inst["name"] or iid
    if inst["state"] != "running":
        raise RuntimeError(f"Instance {label} is {inst['state']}")
    if not inst["public_ip"]:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Instance {label} has no public IP")
    return inst["public_ip"]

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\tools\aws_tools.py ===

"""Read-only AWS tools (L0). Server comes from the ticket via aws.ACTIVE, never from the model."""
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json
import boto3
from langchain_core.tools import tool

from app.tools import aws


def _iid(target: str) -> str:
    iid = aws.ACTIVE.get(target)
    if not iid:
        raise RuntimeError("No server was identified for this ticket.")
    return iid


@tool
def check_ec2_status(target: str) -> dict:
    """EC2 state and status checks (system and instance) of the server. target is "windows" or "linux"."""
    try:
        r = aws._ec2().describe_instance_status(
            InstanceIds=[_iid(target)], IncludeAllInstances=True)["InstanceStatuses"][0]
        return {"state": r["InstanceState"]["Name"],
                "system_status": r["SystemStatus"]["Status"],
                "instance_status": r["InstanceStatus"]["Status"]}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e)[:300]}


@tool
def check_security_group(target: str, port: int) -> dict:
    """Whether the server's security groups allow inbound TCP on a port (e.g. 5985, 22, 3389), and from which sources.
    If the port is not allowed, also lists the inbound ports that are open."""
    try:
        ec2 = aws._ec2()
        inst = ec2.describe_instances(InstanceIds=[_iid(target)])["Reservations"][0]["Instances"][0]
        ids = [g["GroupId"] for g in inst["SecurityGroups"]]
        sources, open_ports = [], set()
        for sg in ec2.describe_security_groups(GroupIds=ids)["SecurityGroups"]:
            for p in sg["IpPermissions"]:
                if p.get("IpProtocol") not in ("-1", "tcp"):
                    continue
                lo, hi = p.get("FromPort", 0), p.get("ToPort", 65535)
                srcs = ([r["CidrIp"] for r in p.get("IpRanges", [])]
                        + [r["CidrIpv6"] for r in p.get("Ipv6Ranges", [])]
                        + [g["GroupId"] for g in p.get("UserIdGroupPairs", [])]
                        + [x["PrefixListId"] for x in p.get("PrefixListIds", [])])
                open_ports.add(f"{lo}-{hi}" if lo != hi else str(lo))
                if lo <= port <= hi:
                    sources += srcs
        out = {"port": port, "allowed": bool(sources), "sources": sources}
        if not sources:
            out["open_inbound_ports"] = sorted(open_ports)
        return out
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e)[:300]}

@tool
def get_cloudwatch_metric(target: str, metric: str = "CPUUtilization", minutes: int = 30) -> dict:
    """Recent CloudWatch EC2 metric for the server (5-minute points). metric is CPUUtilization or StatusCheckFailed."""
    if metric not in ("CPUUtilization", "StatusCheckFailed"):
        return {"error": "metric must be CPUUtilization or StatusCheckFailed"}
    try:
        cw = boto3.client("cloudwatch", region_name=os.getenv("AWS_REGION"), config=aws._CFG)
        end = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        pts = cw.get_metric_statistics(
            Namespace="AWS/EC2", MetricName=metric,
            Dimensions=[{"Name": "InstanceId", "Value": _iid(target)}],
            StartTime=end - timedelta(minutes=min(minutes, 180)), EndTime=end,
            Period=300, Statistics=["Average", "Maximum"])["Datapoints"]
        pts.sort(key=lambda d: d["Timestamp"])
        return {"metric": metric,
                "points": [{"avg": round(p["Average"], 1), "max": round(p["Maximum"], 1)} for p in pts]}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e)[:300]}


@tool
def escalate_l3(number: str, reason: str) -> dict:
    """Page the on-call engineers for a CRITICAL (L3) incident: major outage or anything unsafe to fix automatically.
    This only sends a notification and fixes nothing. Also hand the ticket over with escalate_incident."""
    try:
        lam = boto3.client("lambda", region_name=os.getenv("AWS_REGION"))
        r = lam.invoke(
            FunctionName=os.getenv("L3_LAMBDA", "triage1-l3-notify"),
            Payload=json.dumps({"ticket": number, "reason": reason, "server": dict(aws.ACTIVE)}).encode(),
        )
        if r.get("FunctionError"):
            return {"error": r["Payload"].read().decode()[:300]}
        return {"notified": True, "ticket": number}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e)[:300]}


AWS_TOOLS = [check_ec2_status, check_security_group, get_cloudwatch_metric, escalate_l3]

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\tools\infra.py ===

"""Infrastructure tools for the triage agent: Windows over WinRM, Linux over SSH.

Tools take target="windows" or "linux" (hosts come from .env), never a free-form host.
Tools return {"error": ...} instead of raising, so the agent can read the failure and escalate.
Decisions (threshold checks, resolve vs escalate) are made in code and returned as "next_step",
so a small model only has to follow the instruction.
"""
import json
import os
import re
import socket
import time

import paramiko
import winrm
from langchain_core.tools import tool

from app.tools import aws

TARGETS = ("windows", "linux")
_SAFE_NAME = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._-]{1,64}$")  # blocks shell/PowerShell injection

# Disk verification state (one ticket is worked at a time, so module-level state is safe)
_CLEARED: dict[str, float] = {}   # target -> when temp files were last cleared
_BEFORE: dict[str, dict] = {}     # target -> {disk: used_pct} before the cleanup
VERIFY_WINDOW = 900               # seconds in which a disk check counts as the post-cleanup check

_ERR_STEP = "Call add_work_note with this error, then call escalate_incident."


# ---- config ----
def _threshold() -> float:
    return float(os.getenv("DISK_THRESHOLD_PCT", "85"))


def _service_allowlist() -> set[str]:
    raw = os.getenv("SERVICE_ALLOWLIST",
                    "nginx,docker,postgresql,postgres,sshd,ssh,apache2,httpd,mysql,mysqld,redis,redis-server,cron")
    return {s.strip().lower() for s in raw.split(",") if s.strip()}


# ---- low-level helpers ----
def _host(target: str) -> str:
    if target not in TARGETS:
        raise ValueError(f"target must be one of {TARGETS}")
    ip = aws.current_host(target)  # the instance the current ticket is about, via AWS
    if ip:
        return ip
    if aws.STRICT:
        raise RuntimeError("No server was identified for this ticket, so no server check was run.")
    host = os.getenv("WIN_HOST" if target == "windows" else "LINUX_HOST", "")
    if not host:
        raise RuntimeError(f"No host configured for '{target}' in .env")
    return host


def _port(target: str) -> int:
    return 5985 if target == "windows" else int(os.getenv("LINUX_SSH_PORT", "22"))


def _win(script: str) -> str:
    """Run a PowerShell script on the Windows server over WinRM and return stdout."""
    session = winrm.Session(
        f"http://{_host('windows')}:5985/wsman",
        auth=(os.environ["WIN_USER"], os.environ["WIN_PASSWORD"]),
        transport=os.getenv("WIN_WINRM_TRANSPORT", "ntlm"),
        operation_timeout_sec=20,
        read_timeout_sec=30,
    )
    r = session.run_ps(script)
    if r.status_code != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(r.std_err.decode(errors="replace")[:300])
    return r.std_out.decode(errors="replace").strip()


def _linux(command: str) -> str:
    """Run a shell command on the Linux server over SSH and return stdout."""
    client = paramiko.SSHClient()
    client.set_missing_host_key_policy(paramiko.AutoAddPolicy())
    try:
        client.connect(
            _host("linux"),
            port=_port("linux"),
            username=os.environ["LINUX_USER"],
            password=os.getenv("LINUX_PASSWORD") or None,
            key_filename=os.getenv("LINUX_KEY_PATH") or None,
            timeout=10,
            look_for_keys=False,
            allow_agent=False,
        )
        _, stdout, stderr = client.exec_command(command, timeout=30)
        out = stdout.read().decode(errors="replace").strip()
        code = stdout.channel.recv_exit_status()
        if code != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(stderr.read().decode(errors="replace")[:300] or f"exit code {code}")
        return out
    finally:
        client.close()


def _safe(value: str, what: str) -> str:
    if not _SAFE_NAME.match(value or ""):
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid {what}: only letters, digits, '.', '_' and '-' are allowed")
    return value


_WIN_SVC = ("Get-Service -Name '{svc}' | Select-Object Name,"
            "@{{n='Status';e={{\"$($_.Status)\"}}}} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress")


def _service_state(target: str, svc: str) -> str:
    """'active' when running, else the raw state (inactive, failed, stopped, unknown...)."""
    if target == "windows":
        data = json.loads(_win(_WIN_SVC.format(svc=svc)))
        s = str(data.get("Status", "")).lower()
        return "active" if s == "running" else (s or "unknown")
    # systemctl is-active exits non-zero for a stopped service, so '|| true' keeps the state text
    return (_linux(f"systemctl is-active {svc} || true") or "unknown").strip().lower()


# ---- read-only tools ----
@tool
def check_reachability(target: str) -> dict:
    """Check whether a server is reachable. target is 'windows' or 'linux'.
    Tests the management port (WinRM 5985 for windows, SSH for linux)."""
    try:
        host, port = _host(target), _port(target)
        with socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=5):
            pass
        return {"target": target, "reachable": True, "port": port}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"target": target, "reachable": False, "error": str(e)[:200],
                "next_step": "The server is not reachable. Call add_work_note, then call escalate_incident."}


@tool
def check_disk_usage(target: str) -> dict:
    """Get disk usage for a server. target is 'windows' or 'linux'. Returns each disk with percent used
    and a next_step to follow."""
    try:
        disks = []
        if target == "windows":
            script = (
                "$d = @(Get-CimInstance Win32_LogicalDisk -Filter 'DriveType=3' | "
                "Select-Object DeviceID,"
                "@{n='SizeGB';e={[math]::Round($_.Size/1GB,1)}},"
                "@{n='FreeGB';e={[math]::Round($_.FreeSpace/1GB,1)}}); "
                "ConvertTo-Json -InputObject $d -Compress"
            )
            for d in json.loads(_win(script)):
                size, free = d["SizeGB"], d["FreeGB"]
                disks.append(
                    {"disk": d["DeviceID"], "size_gb": size, "free_gb": free,
                     "used_pct": round(100 * (size - free) / size, 1) if size else None}
                )
        else:
            out = _linux("df -P -x tmpfs -x devtmpfs | tail -n +2")
            for line in out.splitlines():
                p = line.split()
                if len(p) >= 6:
                    disks.append(
                        {"disk": p[5], "size_gb": round(int(p[1]) / 1048576, 1),
                         "free_gb": round(int(p[3]) / 1048576, 1), "used_pct": float(p[4].rstrip("%"))}
                    )
        if not disks:
            raise RuntimeError("No disks were returned by the server.")

        thr = _threshold()
        for d in disks:
            d["over_threshold"] = d["used_pct"] is not None and d["used_pct"] > thr
        over = [d["disk"] for d in disks if d["over_threshold"]]
        res = {"target": target, "threshold_pct": thr, "disks": disks, "over_threshold": over}

        if time.time() - _CLEARED.get(target, 0) < VERIFY_WINDOW:   # this is the post-cleanup check
            _CLEARED.pop(target, None)
            res["before_used_pct"] = _BEFORE.get(target, {})
            if over:
                res["next_step"] = (f"Still over the {thr}% threshold after cleanup ({', '.join(over)}). "
                                    "Call add_work_note with the before and after used_pct, then call escalate_incident.")
            else:
                res["next_step"] = ("Cleanup verified: every disk is now under the threshold. Call add_work_note "
                                    "with the before and after used_pct, then call resolve_incident.")
        else:
            _BEFORE[target] = {d["disk"]: d["used_pct"] for d in disks}
            if over:
                res["next_step"] = (f"Over the {thr}% threshold: {', '.join(over)}. Call clear_temp_files with "
                                    f"target '{target}' (needs engineer approval).")
            else:
                res["next_step"] = ("No disk is over the threshold. Call add_work_note 'no disk issue found', "
                                    "then call resolve_incident.")
        return res
    except Exception as e:
        return {"target": target, "error": str(e)[:300], "next_step": _ERR_STEP}


@tool
def check_service_status(target: str, service_name: str) -> dict:
    """Check whether a service is running. target is 'windows' or 'linux'. Read-only.
    Returns the status and a next_step to follow."""
    try:
        if target not in TARGETS:
            raise ValueError(f"target must be one of {TARGETS}")
        svc = _safe(service_name, "service_name")
        status = _service_state(target, svc)
        if status == "active":
            step = (f"{svc} is already running. Call add_work_note with this result, then call resolve_incident.")
        else:
            step = (f"{svc} is not running (status: {status}). Call start_service with target '{target}' and "
                    f"service_name '{svc}' (needs engineer approval).")
        return {"target": target, "service": svc, "status": status, "next_step": step}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"target": target, "service": service_name, "error": str(e)[:300], "next_step": _ERR_STEP}


@tool
def lookup_ad_user(username: str) -> dict:
    """Look up an Active Directory user by username (sAMAccountName). Returns whether the account is
    enabled, locked out, or has an expired password, and a next_step to follow. Use it for access issues."""
    try:
        u = _safe(username, "username")
        script = (
            "Import-Module ActiveDirectory; "
            f"Get-ADUser -Identity '{u}' -Properties Enabled,LockedOut,PasswordExpired,LastLogonDate | "
            "Select-Object SamAccountName,Enabled,LockedOut,PasswordExpired,"
            "@{n='LastLogon';e={\"$($_.LastLogonDate)\"}} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress"
        )
        res = json.loads(_win(script))
        if not res.get("Enabled"):
            step = "The account is disabled and needs L2 review. Call add_work_note, then call escalate_incident."
        elif res.get("LockedOut"):
            step = (f"The account is locked out. Call unlock_ad_account with username '{u}' "
                    "(needs engineer approval). Do not resolve the ticket yet.")
        elif res.get("PasswordExpired"):
            step = "The password is expired, not locked. Call add_work_note, then call escalate_incident."
        else:
            step = "The account is enabled and not locked out. Call add_work_note with this, then call resolve_incident."
        res["next_step"] = step
        return res
    except Exception as e:
        return {"username": username, "error": str(e)[:300], "next_step": _ERR_STEP}


# ---- writes (these need human approval, see policy.py) ----
@tool
def clear_temp_files(target: str) -> dict:
    """Delete temp files older than 1 day on a server to free disk space. target is 'windows' or 'linux'.
    Needs engineer approval. Afterwards call check_disk_usage to verify."""
    try:
        if target == "windows":
            script = (
                "$f = @(Get-ChildItem -Path $env:TEMP,'C:\\Windows\\Temp' -Recurse -Force -File "
                "-ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { $_.LastWriteTime -lt (Get-Date).AddDays(-1) }); "
                "$b = ($f | Measure-Object Length -Sum).Sum; "
                "$f | Remove-Item -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; "
                "Write-Output ([math]::Round($b/1MB,1))"
            )
            res = {"target": target, "freed_mb": float(_win(script) or 0)}
        else:
            out = _linux("find /tmp -xdev -type f -mtime +1 -delete -print | wc -l")
            res = {"target": target, "files_deleted": int(out or 0)}
        _CLEARED[target] = time.time()
        res["next_step"] = f"Cleanup done. Call check_disk_usage with target '{target}' to verify."
        return res
    except Exception as e:
        return {"target": target, "error": str(e)[:300], "next_step": _ERR_STEP}


@tool
def start_service(target: str, service_name: str) -> dict:
    """Start a stopped service. target is 'windows' or 'linux'. Needs engineer approval.
    Returns the new status and a next_step to follow."""
    try:
        if target not in TARGETS:
            raise ValueError(f"target must be one of {TARGETS}")
        svc = _safe(service_name, "service_name")
        if svc.lower() not in _service_allowlist():
            raise ValueError(f"Service '{svc}' is not on the allowed list (SERVICE_ALLOWLIST).")
        if target == "windows":
            _win(f"Start-Service -Name '{svc}'")
        else:
            _linux(f"sudo -n systemctl start {svc}")
        status = _service_state(target, svc)
        if status == "active":
            step = (f"{svc} is now running. Call add_work_note with before and after status, "
                    "then call resolve_incident.")
        else:
            step = (f"{svc} is still not running (status: {status}). Call add_work_note with this, "
                    "then call escalate_incident.")
        return {"target": target, "service": svc, "status": status, "next_step": step}
    except Exception as e:
        return {"target": target, "service": service_name, "error": str(e)[:300], "next_step": _ERR_STEP}


@tool
def unlock_ad_account(username: str) -> dict:
    """Unlock a locked Active Directory account. Needs engineer approval before it runs."""
    try:
        u = _safe(username, "username")
        script = (
            "Import-Module ActiveDirectory; "
            f"Unlock-ADAccount -Identity '{u}'; "
            f"Get-ADUser -Identity '{u}' -Properties LockedOut | "
            "Select-Object SamAccountName,LockedOut | ConvertTo-Json -Compress"
        )
        res = json.loads(_win(script))
        if res.get("LockedOut"):
            res["next_step"] = "The account is still locked. Call add_work_note, then call escalate_incident."
        else:
            res["next_step"] = ("The account is now unlocked. Call add_work_note with before and after "
                                "LockedOut values, then call resolve_incident.")
        return res
    except Exception as e:
        return {"username": username, "error": str(e)[:300], "next_step": _ERR_STEP}


INFRA_TOOLS = [check_reachability, check_disk_usage, check_service_status, lookup_ad_user,
               clear_temp_files, start_service, unlock_ad_account]
HIGH_RISK_TOOLS = {"unlock_ad_account"}

"""ServiceNow tools for the triage agent.

Each function decorated with @tool is something the LangChain agent can call.
The docstring is what the LLM reads to decide when to use it, so keep it short.
"""
import os
import requests
from langchain_core.tools import tool
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone

STATE_IN_PROGRESS = 2  # "WIP"
STATE_RESOLVED = 6

# Only return these fields so we don't waste tokens on the 80+ others
FIELDS = "number,short_description,description,state,priority,caller_id,assignment_group,assigned_to,contact_type,work_notes"


# ---- config (read at call time so load_dotenv() has already run) ----
def _caller() -> str:
    return os.getenv("SNOW_CALLER", "sre_triage_agent")


def _group() -> str:
    return os.getenv("SNOW_ASSIGNMENT_GROUP", "L1-SRE-Agent")


def _assignee() -> str:
    return os.getenv("SNOW_ASSIGNEE", "svc_triage_01")


RETRY_METHODS = {"GET", "PATCH", "PUT"}  # POST (create) is never retried: it could duplicate a ticket


def _request(method: str, path: str, identity: str = "agent", **kwargs):
    """Single place that talks to SNOW. Retries transient failures on safe methods.
    Raises with the response body on errors."""
    base = os.environ["SNOW_INSTANCE"].strip().rstrip("/")
    if not base.startswith("http"):
        base = f"https://{base}.service-now.com" if "." not in base else f"https://{base}"
    if identity == "checker":
        auth = (os.environ["SNOW_CHECKER_USER"], os.environ["SNOW_CHECKER_PASSWORD"])
    else:
        auth = (os.environ["SNOW_USER"], os.environ["SNOW_PASSWORD"])
    attempts = 3 if method.upper() in RETRY_METHODS else 1
    resp = None
    for i in range(attempts):
        try:
            resp = requests.request(
                method,
                f"{base}/api/now/table/{path}",
                auth=auth,
                headers={"Accept": "application/json", "Content-Type": "application/json"},
                timeout=(5, 20),
                **kwargs,
            )
        except (requests.ConnectionError, requests.Timeout) as e:
            if i == attempts - 1:
                raise RuntimeError(f"SNOW unreachable: {str(e)[:200]}")
            time.sleep(2 ** i)
            continue
        if resp.status_code in (429, 502, 503, 504) and i < attempts - 1:
            time.sleep(2 ** i)
            continue
        break
    if not resp.ok:
        raise RuntimeError(f"SNOW {resp.status_code} on {method} {path}: {resp.text[:500]}")
    return resp.json()["result"]

def _lookup_sys_id(table: str, query: str, label: str) -> str:
    rows = _request(
        "GET", table, params={"sysparm_query": query, "sysparm_limit": 1, "sysparm_fields": "sys_id"}
    )
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError(f"{label} not found in SNOW")
    return rows[0]["sys_id"]


def _user_id(user: str) -> str:
    """Caller can be a ServiceNow user_name or an email address."""
    user = user.replace("^", "")
    return _lookup_sys_id("sys_user", f"user_name={user}^ORemail={user}", f"User '{user}'")

def _group_id(name: str) -> str:
    return _lookup_sys_id("sys_user_group", f"name={name}", f"Group '{name}'")


def _incident_id(number: str) -> str:
    return _lookup_sys_id("incident", f"number={number}", f"Incident {number}")


def _flatten(record: dict) -> dict:
    """Reference fields come back as {'display_value':..., 'link':...}. Keep just the readable value."""
    return {k: (v.get("display_value") if isinstance(v, dict) else v) for k, v in record.items()}


def _fetch(number: str) -> dict:
    rows = _request(
        "GET",
        "incident",
        params={
            "sysparm_query": f"number={number}",
            "sysparm_limit": 1,
            "sysparm_fields": FIELDS,
            "sysparm_display_value": "true",
        },
    )
    if not rows:
        return {"error": f"Incident {number} not found"}
    return _flatten(rows[0])


# ---- tools the agent can call ----
@tool
def get_incident(number: str) -> dict:
    """Fetch a ServiceNow incident by number (e.g. INC0010001). Returns its key fields."""
    return _fetch(number)

def search_users(text: str, limit: int = 8) -> list[dict]:
    """Type-ahead for the Caller field: active users matching name, user name or email."""
    text = text.replace("^", " ").strip()[:50]
    rows = _request("GET", "sys_user", params={
        "sysparm_query": f"active=true^nameLIKE{text}^ORuser_nameLIKE{text}^ORemailLIKE{text}",
        "sysparm_limit": limit,
        "sysparm_fields": "user_name,name,email",
    })
    return [{"user_name": r.get("user_name", ""), "name": r.get("name", ""), "email": r.get("email", "")}
            for r in rows]


def find_ci_id(name: str) -> str | None:
    """sys_id of the CMDB configuration item with this name, or None if there is none."""
    try:
        rows = _request("GET", "cmdb_ci", params={
            "sysparm_query": f"name={name.replace('^', ' ')}", "sysparm_limit": 1, "sysparm_fields": "sys_id"})
        return rows[0]["sys_id"] if rows else None
    except Exception:
        return None


@tool
def create_incident(short_description: str, description: str, urgency: int = 3, impact: int = 3,
                    caller: str = "", ci: str = "") -> dict:
    """Create a ServiceNow incident, assign it to the triage agent in the L1 group, and move it to
    In Progress. urgency and impact: 1=high, 2=medium, 3=low. caller is a ServiceNow user_name
    (defaults to the integration user). ci is the configuration item (server) name.
    Returns the incident as it now stands."""
    body = {
        "short_description": short_description,
        "description": description,
        "urgency": str(urgency),
        "impact": str(impact),
        "contact_type": os.getenv("SNOW_CHANNEL", "virtual_agent"),
        "caller_id": _user_id(caller or _caller()),
        "assignment_group": _group_id(_group()),
        "assigned_to": _user_id(_assignee()),
    }
    if ci:
        ci_id = find_ci_id(ci)
        if ci_id:
            body["cmdb_ci"] = ci_id
    created = _request("POST", "incident", json=body)
    # Assignment is done. Now move to WIP as a separate step.
    _request(
        "PATCH",
        f"incident/{created['sys_id']}",
        json={"state": str(STATE_IN_PROGRESS), "work_notes": "Assigned to triage agent. Work started."},
    )
    return _fetch(created["number"])
    # Assignment is done. Now move to WIP as a separate step.
    _request(
        "PATCH",
        f"incident/{created['sys_id']}",
        json={"state": str(STATE_IN_PROGRESS), "work_notes": "Assigned to triage agent. Work started."},
    )
    return _fetch(created["number"])

@tool
def claim_incident(number: str, work_note: str = "Triage agent picked up this ticket.") -> dict:
    """Take ownership of an existing incident: assign it to the triage agent and move it to In Progress.
    Use this first when a ticket arrives in the L1 queue."""
    sys_id = _incident_id(number)
    _request("PATCH", f"incident/{sys_id}", json={"assigned_to": _user_id(_assignee())})
    _request(
        "PATCH",
        f"incident/{sys_id}",
        json={"state": str(STATE_IN_PROGRESS), "work_notes": work_note},
    )
    return _fetch(number)


@tool
def resolve_incident(number: str, close_notes: str) -> dict:
    """Resolve an incident. close_notes must describe what was fixed. Only call after the underlying issue is fixed."""
    sys_id = _incident_id(number)
    close_code = os.getenv("SNOW_CLOSE_CODE", "Solution provided")

    # Save the resolution fields first, then change the state.
    saved = _request(
        "PATCH",
        f"incident/{sys_id}",
        params={"sysparm_fields": "close_code"},
        json={"close_code": close_code, "close_notes": close_notes},
    )
    # SNOW silently ignores invalid choice values, so check the write really stuck.
    if not saved.get("close_code"):
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"SNOW did not accept close_code '{close_code}'. "
            "Use a value from the Resolution code dropdown and set SNOW_CLOSE_CODE."
        )

    _request("PATCH", f"incident/{sys_id}", json={"state": str(STATE_RESOLVED)})
    return _fetch(number)

@tool
def add_work_note(number: str, note: str) -> dict:
    """Add a work note to an incident. Use it to record checks done, findings, and actions taken."""
    _request("PATCH", f"incident/{_incident_id(number)}", json={"work_notes": note})
    return {"number": number, "note_added": True}

@tool
def escalate_incident(number: str, reason: str) -> dict:
    """Hand an incident to the human L2 team when it is beyond L1, could not be fixed, or needs approval.
    Unassigns the agent and records the reason. The L2 team is fixed by configuration: do not pass a group."""
    group = os.getenv("SNOW_ESCALATION_GROUP", "")
    if not group:
        raise RuntimeError("SNOW_ESCALATION_GROUP is not set in .env")
    _request(
        "PATCH",
        f"incident/{_incident_id(number)}",
        json={
            "assignment_group": _group_id(group),
            "assigned_to": "",
            "work_notes": f"Escalated by triage agent. Reason: {reason}",
        },
    )
    return _fetch(number)

def list_queue() -> list:
    """Ticket numbers waiting in the L1 queue: New, unassigned, oldest first."""
    rows = _request(
        "GET",
        "incident",
        params={
            "sysparm_query": f"active=true^state=1^assigned_toISEMPTY"
                             f"^assignment_group.name={_group()}^ORDERBYpriority^ORDERBYsys_created_on",
            "sysparm_fields": "number",
            "sysparm_limit": 10,
        },
    )
    return [r["number"] for r in rows]

# Used only when ServiceNow has no SLA records on the ticket. (response_min, resolution_min) by priority.
# These numbers are placeholders. Replace them with your real SLAs.
SLA_FALLBACK = {"1": (15, 240), "2": (30, 480), "3": (120, 1440), "4": (240, 4320)}


def _human(minutes: float) -> str:
    m = max(int(round(minutes)), 1)
    if m < 60:
        return f"{m} minute{'s' if m != 1 else ''}"
    h, rem = divmod(m, 60)
    if h < 24:
        return f"{h} hour{'s' if h != 1 else ''}" + (f" {rem} minutes" if rem else "")
    d, h = divmod(h, 24)
    return f"{d} day{'s' if d != 1 else ''}" + (f" {h} hours" if h else "")


def get_sla(number: str) -> dict:
    """Response and resolution targets as readable text. Reads the ticket's task_sla records
    (SLA names containing 'response' / 'resolution'); falls back to SLA_FALLBACK by priority."""
    out = {"response": None, "resolution": None, "source": "servicenow"}
    try:
        rows = _request("GET", "task_sla", params={
            "sysparm_query": f"task={_incident_id(number)}",
            "sysparm_fields": "sla.name,planned_end_time",
            "sysparm_limit": 10,
        })
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        for r in rows:
            name = str(r.get("sla.name", "")).lower()
            end = r.get("planned_end_time")
            if not end:
                continue
            due = datetime.strptime(end, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S").replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            if due <= now:
                continue
            text = _human((due - now).total_seconds() / 60)
            if "response" in name and not out["response"]:
                out["response"] = text
            elif "resolution" in name and not out["resolution"]:
                out["resolution"] = text
    except Exception:
        pass
    if not (out["response"] and out["resolution"]):
        try:
            prio = (_fetch(number).get("priority") or "3")[:1]
        except Exception:
            prio = "3"
        resp_m, reso_m = SLA_FALLBACK.get(prio, SLA_FALLBACK["3"])
        out["response"] = out["response"] or _human(resp_m)
        out["resolution"] = out["resolution"] or _human(reso_m)
        out["source"] = "fallback"
    return out

# The agent will import this list in Phase 3
SNOW_TOOLS = [get_incident, create_incident, claim_incident, add_work_note, escalate_incident, resolve_incident]

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\auth.py ===

"""Authentication + role enforcement using Supabase sessions."""
import hashlib
import logging
import time
from fastapi import Request
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse, Response

from app import store

log = logging.getLogger("uvicorn.error")
LEVELS = {"viewer": 1, "approver": 2, "admin": 3}

import re

_RUN_ONE = re.compile(r"^/api/agent/runs/[^/]+$")

def required_role(method: str, path: str) -> str:
    # Admin only
    if (path.startswith(("/api/admin", "/admin", "/api/users", "/api/agent/audit"))
            or path == "/api/agent/auto"
            or (path.startswith("/api/rag") and method not in ("GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"))):
        return "admin"
    # Approver and above: Agent Console, KB pages, run lists, queue, decisions
    if path in ("/agent", "/knowledge") or path.startswith("/api/rag"):
        return "approver"
    if path.startswith("/api/agent/"):
        # Chat users still poll their own run and the live banner
        if method == "GET" and (_RUN_ONE.match(path) or path == "/api/agent/live"):
            return "viewer"
        return "approver"
    return "viewer"

CACHE_TTL = 45   # seconds a session lookup is reused; also the longest a disabled user keeps access
_CACHE: dict[str, tuple[float, float, dict]] = {}   # token_hash -> (cached_at, session_expires, user_info)


def _lookup_session(token: str | None) -> dict | None:
    if not token:
        return None
    token_hash = hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest()
    now = time.time()
    hit = _CACHE.get(token_hash)
    if hit and now - hit[0] < CACHE_TTL and now < hit[1]:
        return hit[2]
    res = store.supabase.table("sessions").select("user_id, expires_at").eq("token_hash", token_hash).execute()
    if not res.data or res.data[0]["expires_at"] <= now:
        _CACHE.pop(token_hash, None)
        return None
    ures = store.supabase.table("users").select("user_id, name, role, status").eq("user_id", res.data[0]["user_id"]).execute()
    if not ures.data or ures.data[0].get("status") != "active":
        _CACHE.pop(token_hash, None)
        return None
    _CACHE[token_hash] = (now, res.data[0]["expires_at"], ures.data[0])
    return ures.data[0]


def forget_session(token: str | None):
    if token:
        _CACHE.pop(hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest(), None)


def forget_user(user_id: str):
    """Call after a role change or disable so it applies immediately (used by the admin console later)."""
    for k in [k for k, v in _CACHE.items() if v[2]["user_id"] == user_id]:
        _CACHE.pop(k, None)

class AuthMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next):
        path = request.url.path
        if path in ("/api/health", "/api/auth/login") or request.method == "OPTIONS" or path.startswith("/static"):
            return await call_next(request)

        user_info = _lookup_session(request.cookies.get("triage_session"))

        if not user_info:
            if path.startswith("/api/"):
                return JSONResponse({"detail": "Unauthorized"}, status_code=401)
            return Response(status_code=302, headers={"Location": "/static/login.html"})

        role = user_info["role"]
        need = required_role(request.method, path)
        
        if LEVELS.get(role, 0) < LEVELS.get(need, 1):
            store.audit(None, None, user_info["user_id"], "forbidden", f"{request.method} {path} needs {need}")
            return JSONResponse({"detail": f"This action requires the {need} role."}, status_code=403)

        # Redirect Admin away from the chat interface (Phase 1 rule)
        if path == "/" and role == "admin":
            return Response(status_code=302, headers={"Location": "/admin"})

        request.state.user = user_info["user_id"]
        request.state.role = role
        request.state.user_name = user_info["name"]

        return await call_next(request)

=== FILE: C:\Users\VedaVidyadaran\sre-triage-agent\app\checker.py ===

"""P5 checker: independent, deterministic verification of tickets TRIAGE-1 resolved.

Maker-checker: the checker uses its own ServiceNow account (SNOW_CHECKER_USER) and only read-only
infra tools. No LLM is involved: pass or fail is a rule applied to a fresh check.

verify_run() is called from app/api/agent.py::_consume while the run still holds the active slot,
because the infra tools find the server through aws.ACTIVE and the next run would clear it.

CHECKER_MODE: off | shadow (default: verify and write a work note, change nothing) | on (close or reopen).
"""
import logging
import os
import time
import uuid

from app import store
from app.agent.report import outcome_code
from app.tools.snow import _request

log = logging.getLogger("triage.checker")
ACTOR = "triage-checker"
STATE_RESOLVED, STATE_IN_PROGRESS, STATE_CLOSED = "6", "2", "7"


def mode() -> str:
    m = os.getenv("CHECKER_MODE", "shadow").strip().lower()
    return m if m in ("off", "shadow", "on") else "shadow"


def _soak() -> float:
    return float(os.getenv("VERIFY_SOAK_SECONDS", "60"))


# ---- ServiceNow, as the checker identity ----
def _snow(method: str, path: str, **kw):
    return _request(method, path, identity="checker", **kw)


def _sys_id(table: str, query: str) -> str:
    rows = _snow("GET", table, params={"sysparm_query": query, "sysparm_limit": 1, "sysparm_fields": "sys_id"})
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError(f"Not found in ServiceNow: {table} {query}")
    return rows[0]["sys_id"]


def _ticket(number: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """(sys_id, raw state code) read with the checker's own account."""
    rows = _snow("GET", "incident", params={
        "sysparm_query": f"number={number}", "sysparm_limit": 1,
        "sysparm_fields": "sys_id,state", "sysparm_display_value": "false"})
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError(f"Incident {number} not found")
    return rows[0]["sys_id"], str(rows[0]["state"])


def _note(sys_id: str, text: str, **fields) -> None:
    _snow("PATCH", f"incident/{sys_id}", json={**fields, "work_notes": text[:8000]})


def _set_state(number: str, sys_id: str, state: str, text: str, **fields) -> None:
    """Changes the state and re-reads it: ServiceNow can silently ignore a write the account may not make."""
    _note(sys_id, text, state=state, **fields)
    _, now = _ticket(number)
    if now != state:
        try:
            _note(sys_id, f"{ACTOR}: ServiceNow did not accept the change to state {state}; the ticket is still "
                          f"{now}. Disregard the note above.")
        except Exception:
            pass
        raise RuntimeError(f"ServiceNow ignored the state change (still {now}). Check the checker's roles and ACLs.")


# ---- what to verify, taken from what the agent actually did ----
def _last_call(run: dict, *names: str) -> dict:
    for e in reversed(run.get("events", [])):
        if e.get("type") == "tool_call" and e.get("name") in names:
            return e.get("args") or {}
    return {}


def _spec(run: dict):
    """(kind, args) for the skill this run used, or None when there is nothing to verify."""
    skill = run.get("skill") or ""
    if skill in ("linux-service-down", "windows-service-down"):
        a = _last_call(run, "start_service", "check_service_status")
        if a.get("service_name") and a.get("target"):
            return "service", {"target": a["target"], "service_name": a["service_name"]}
    elif skill in ("linux-disk-full", "windows-disk-full"):
        a = _last_call(run, "check_disk_usage")
        return "disk", {"target": a.get("target") or ("linux" if skill.startswith("linux") else "windows")}
    elif skill == "ad-account-locked":
        a = _last_call(run, "lookup_ad_user", "unlock_ad_account")
        if a.get("username"):
            return "ad", {"username": a["username"]}
    return None


# ---- the checks: (True | False | None = could not check, evidence text) ----
def _check(kind: str, args: dict):
    from app.tools.infra import check_disk_usage, check_service_status, lookup_ad_user   # lazy: heavy imports
    if kind == "service":
        r = check_service_status.invoke(args)
        if r.get("error"):
            return None, f"could not check {args['service_name']}: {r['error']}"
        return r.get("status") == "active", f"{args['service_name']} on {args['target']} is {r.get('status')}"
    if kind == "disk":
        r = check_disk_usage.invoke(args)
        if r.get("error"):
            return None, f"could not check disk on {args['target']}: {r['error']}"
        disks = ", ".join(f"{d.get('disk')} {d.get('used_pct')}%" for d in r.get("disks", []))
        return not r.get("over_threshold"), f"disk on {args['target']}: {disks} (limit {r.get('threshold_pct')}%)"
    if kind == "ad":
        r = lookup_ad_user.invoke(args)
        if r.get("error"):
            return None, f"could not look up {args['username']}: {r['error']}"
        ok = bool(r.get("Enabled")) and not r.get("LockedOut")
        return ok, f"AD user {args['username']}: enabled={bool(r.get('Enabled'))}, locked out={bool(r.get('LockedOut'))}"
    return None, f"unknown check kind {kind}"


def _run_checks(kind: str, args: dict):
    """Services are checked twice, VERIFY_SOAK_SECONDS apart, to catch a service that comes up and dies again."""
    notes = []
    for i in range(2 if kind == "service" else 1):
        if i:
            time.sleep(_soak())
        ok, text = _check(kind, args)
        notes.append(text)
        if ok is not True:
            return ok, notes
    return True, notes


# ---- bookkeeping ----
def _audit(run: dict, action: str, detail: str = "") -> None:
    try:
        store.audit(run["id"], run["ticket"], ACTOR, action, detail)
    except Exception as e:
        log.warning("checker audit failed: %s", str(e)[:200])


def _record(run: dict, md: str, verdict: str, notes: list, reopen_count: int, closed: bool) -> None:
    try:
        store.supabase.table("verifications").insert({
            "id": uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], "ticket": run["ticket"], "run_id": run["id"], "ts": time.time(),
            "mode": md, "verdict": verdict, "checks_json": notes, "reopen_count": reopen_count,
            "closed_at": time.time() if closed else None}).execute()
    except Exception as e:
        log.warning("could not save verification for %s: %s", run["ticket"], str(e)[:200])


def _reopens(ticket: str) -> int:
    try:
        rows = (store.supabase.table("verifications").select("id").eq("ticket", ticket)
                .eq("verdict", "fail").eq("mode", "on").execute().data)
        return len(rows or [])
    except Exception:
        return 0


# ---- the consolidated report ----
def _report(run: dict, kind: str, notes: list, verdict: str, comment: str, prefix: str = "") -> str:
    """One work note: what TRIAGE-1 ran, what the checker ran, the checker's comment and the verdict."""
    from app.agent.report import _clock, describe          # same wording the run report and UI use
    lines = [f"{prefix}TRIAGE-1 RESOLUTION REPORT (checker)", "",
             f"Ticket:   {run['ticket']}", f"Server:   {run.get('server') or 'unknown'}",
             f"Playbook: {run.get('skill') or 'none'}", "",
             "1. Actions by the TRIAGE-1 agent (times UTC):"]
    n, final = 0, ""
    for e in run.get("events", []):
        if e.get("type") == "verification":
            continue
        if e.get("type") == "final":
            final = str(e.get("text", "")).strip()
            continue
        d = describe(e)
        if d:
            n += 1
            lines.append(f"   {n}. {_clock(e.get('ts'))}  {d}")
    if not n:
        lines.append("   (no steps recorded)")
    if final:
        lines += ["", f"   Agent summary: {final[:400]}"]
    lines += ["", f"2. Independent checks by {ACTOR} ({kind}):"]
    for i, t in enumerate(notes, 1):
        lines.append(f"   Check {i}" + (f" (after {_soak():g}s)" if i > 1 else "") + f": {t}")
    lines += ["", f"3. Checker comment: {comment}", f"   Verdict: {verdict.upper()}"]
    return "\n".join(lines)


# ---- entry point ----
def verify_run(run: dict, emit=lambda text, verdict=None: None):
    """Verifies a run that ended 'resolved'. Returns 'pass', 'fail', 'unverified' or None (nothing done).
    emit(text, verdict) adds a step to the run. In shadow mode the verdict is prefixed 'shadow-'."""
    md = mode()

        return None
    spec = _spec(run)
    if not spec:
        _audit(run, "verify_skipped", f"no verification spec for skill {run.get('skill')}")
        return None
    kind, args = spec
    ticket = run["ticket"]
    tag = "" if md == "on" else "shadow-"
    shadow = "" if md == "on" else "[shadow] "
    try:
        sys_id, state = _ticket(ticket)
    except Exception as e:
        _audit(run, "verify_error", str(e)[:300])
        return None
    if state != STATE_RESOLVED:
        _audit(run, "verify_skipped", f"ticket state is {state}, not Resolved")
        return None
    emit("Checker: verifying the fix independently")
    ok, notes = _run_checks(kind, args)
    evidence = "; ".join(notes)
    try:
        if ok is True:
            verdict = "pass"
            comment = ("Fix confirmed by an independent re-check. "
                       + ("Moving the ticket from Resolved to Closed." if md == "on"
                          else "Would move the ticket fromd

"""P1 declaration: proposal, approver sign-off, ServiceNow update, bridge link and one disruption notice."""
import logging
import os
import re
import smtplib
import time
import uuid
from email.message import EmailMessage
from email.utils import make_msgid

from app import store
from app.tools.snow import _request, _incident_id, add_work_note

log = logging.getLogger("triage.escalation")
EMAIL = re.compile(r"^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$")
UPDATE_MIN = int(os.getenv("P1_UPDATE_MIN", "30"))


def conf() -> dict:
    if os.getenv("NOTICE_TRANSPORT", "smtp").lower() == "sns":
        return {"bridge": store.get_setting("bridge_link", "") or "",
                "to": ["All confirmed subscribers of the SNS topic"], "cc": []}
    return {"bridge": store.get_setting("bridge_link", "") or "",
            "to": store.get_setting("p1_notice_to", []) or [], "cc": store.get_setting("p1_notice_cc", []) or []}


def touches_critical(run: dict) -> bool:
    from app.agent.policy import _critical
    crit = _critical()
    return any(str((e.get("args") or {}).get("service_name", "")).lower() in crit
               for e in run.get("events", []) if e.get("type") == "tool_call")


def draft(run: dict) -> str:
    c = conf()
    started = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC", time.gmtime(run.get("started", time.time())))
    return (f"Incident {run['ticket']} has been declared a P1.\n\n"
            f"Affected server: {run.get('server') or 'see ticket'}\nStarted: {started}\n"
            f"Bridge: {c['bridge'] or '(not configured)'}\nNext update: within {UPDATE_MIN} minutes.\n\n"
            "This notice states confirmed facts only. Cause and ETA will follow once known.")


def propose(run: dict, reason: str) -> None:
    if run.get("p1") or not touches_critical(run):
        return
    run["p1"] = {"status": "proposed", "reason": reason, "draft": draft(run), "at": time.time()}
    store.audit(run["id"], run["ticket"], "system", "p1_proposed", reason)


def view(run: dict) -> dict | None:
    p = run.get("p1")
    if not p:
        return None
    c = conf()
    return {"status": p["status"], "reason": p["reason"], "draft": p["draft"], "to": c["to"], "cc": c["cc"],
            "bridge": c["bridge"], "notice_error": p.get("notice_error", "")}


def _send(to: list, cc: list, subject: str, body: str) -> str:
    """Picks the transport from NOTICE_TRANSPORT (smtp | ses | sns). Returns a message id."""
    mode = os.getenv("NOTICE_TRANSPORT", "smtp").lower()
    if mode == "ses":
        return _send_ses(to, cc, subject, body)
    if mode == "sns":
        return _send_sns(subject, body)
    return _send_smtp(to, cc, subject, body)


def _send_smtp(to: list, cc: list, subject: str, body: str) -> str:
    host = os.getenv("SMTP_HOST")
    if not host:
        raise RuntimeError("SMTP_HOST is not set in .env")
    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg["From"] = os.getenv("SMTP_FROM") or os.getenv("SMTP_USER", "")
    msg["To"] = ", ".join(to)
    if cc:
        msg["Cc"] = ", ".join(cc)
    msg["Subject"] = subject
    msg["Message-ID"] = make_msgid()
    msg.set_content(body)
    with smtplib.SMTP(host, int(os.getenv("SMTP_PORT", "587")), timeout=20) as s:
        s.starttls()
        if os.getenv("SMTP_USER"):
            s.login(os.getenv("SMTP_USER"), os.getenv("SMTP_PASSWORD", ""))
        s.send_message(msg, to_addrs=to + cc)
    return msg["Message-ID"]


def _send_ses(to: list, cc: list, subject: str, body: str) -> str:
    import boto3
    sender = os.getenv("SES_FROM")
    if not sender:
        raise RuntimeError("SES_FROM is not set in .env")
    client = boto3.client("ses", region_name=os.getenv("AWS_REGION"))
    dest = {"ToAddresses": to}
    if cc:
        dest["CcAddresses"] = cc
    r = client.send_email(Source=sender, Destination=dest,
                          Message={"Subject": {"Data": subject[:200]}, "Body": {"Text": {"Data": body}}})
    return r["MessageId"]


def _send_sns(subject: str, body: str) -> str:
    """SNS delivers to the topic's subscribers, so the To and Cc lists on the card are not used."""
    import boto3
    topic = os.getenv("SNS_TOPIC_ARN")
    if not topic:
        raise RuntimeError("SNS_TOPIC_ARN is not set in .env")
    client = boto3.client("sns", region_name=os.getenv("AWS_REGION"))
    r = client.publish(TopicArn=topic, Subject=subject[:100], Message=body)
    return r["MessageId"]


def declare(run: dict, approver: str, body: str) -> None:
    """Idempotent: ServiceNow is updated once, the email is sent once (retries only when the send failed)."""
    p, c, inc = run["p1"], conf(), run["ticket"]
    p["draft"] = body
    if not p.get("snow_done"):
        _request("PATCH", f"incident/{_incident_id(inc)}", json={
            "impact": "1", "urgency": "1",
            "work_notes": f"P1 declared by {approver}. Reason: {p['reason']}. Bridge: {c['bridge'] or 'not configured'}"})
        p["snow_done"] = True
        store.audit(run["id"], inc, approver, "p1_declared", p["reason"])
    done = store.supabase.table("notices").select("id").eq("incident", inc).eq("type", "p1_declared").execute().data
    if done:
        p["status"], p["notice_error"] = "declared", ""
        return
    try:
        if not c["to"]:
            raise RuntimeError("No notice recipients configured (Admin page)")
        mid = _send(c["to"], c["cc"], f"[P1] {inc} - major incident declared", body)
        store.supabase.table("notices").insert({
            "id": uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], "incident": inc, "run_id": run["id"], "type": "p1_declared",
            "sent_at": time.time(), "approver": approver, "message_id": mid,
            "recipients_json": {"to": c["to"], "cc": c["cc"]}, "body": body}).execute()
        add_work_note.invoke({"number": inc, "note": f"Disruption notice emailed to {', '.join(c['to'] + c['cc'])}."})
        store.audit(run["id"], inc, approver, "p1_notice_sent", ", ".join(c["to"]))
        p["status"], p["notice_error"] = "declared", ""
    except Exception as e:
        p["status"], p["notice_error"] = "declared_notice_failed", str(e)[:200]
        store.audit(run["id"], inc, approver, "p1_notice_failed", p["notice_error"])

# --- Infrastructure Clients ---

    max_connection_lifetime=180  # AuraDB drops idle connections; recycle before that

# --- Log Truncation Globals ---
MAX_FILE_CHARS = 6000
SIGNAL_PATTERN = re.compile(r'(ERROR|CRITICAL|FATAL|WARN|Exception|Traceback)', re.IGNORECASE)

# --- Request Validation Limits ---
MAX_INPUT_CHARS = 200_000        # one chat message / attached file (truncated later by smart_truncate_log)
MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES = 30        # 15 turns x 2 messages = the frontend slider's maximum
MAX_FIELD_CHARS = 200            # graph entity / relation names (matches the CSV import limit)

# --- Graph Extraction Globals ---
EXTRACT_WINDOW_CHARS = 2500      # characters of document sent to the LLM per call
EXTRACT_MAX_WINDOWS = 4          # max LLM calls per document
MAX_TRIPLES_PER_DOC = 60         # hard cap on edges one document can create
MAX_FIELD_LEN = 80               # entity / relation names longer than this are dropped

EXTRACT_PROMPT = """You extract knowledge-graph triples from SRE documents (runbooks, SOPs, incident reports).
Return ONLY a JSON array of [subject, relation, object] triples. No prose, no markdown, no code fences.
Rules:
- subject and object are short canonical names (1-4 words) of services, components, error messages, alerts, tools, commands, causes or fixes. Keep the exact casing of technical terms (e.g. OOMKilled, kafka-broker, HikariPool).
- relation is a short lowercase verb phrase such as "is caused by", "is resolved by", "depends on", "is diagnosed with", "is a symptom of", "runs on", "triggers", "mitigates".
- Only state facts that are explicitly in the text. Maximum 15 triples.
Example output: [["CrashLoopBackOff","is caused by","OOMKilled"],["OOMKilled","is resolved by","Raising Memory Limit"]]"""


# ======================================================================
# REQUEST MODELS (validated by FastAPI; bad input returns HTTP 422)
# ======================================================================
GraphName = Annotated[str, StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=1, max_length=MAX_FIELD_CHARS)]


class ChatMessage(BaseModel):
    """One past turn. The client may only send user/assistant roles, never 'system'."""
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    role: Literal["user", "assistant"]
    content: str = Field(max_length=MAX_INPUT_CHARS)


class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
    query: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=MAX_INPUT_CHARS)
    history: list[ChatMessage] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=MAX_HISTORY_MESSAGES)

    @field_validator("query")
    @classmethod
    def query_not_blank(cls, v: str) -> str:
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError("query must not be blank")
        return v


class GraphEdge(BaseModel):
    src: GraphName
    rel: GraphName
    dst: GraphName


def smart_truncate_log(text: str, max_chars: int = MAX_FILE_CHARS) -> str:
    """Extracts signal lines from massive logs using regex to prevent LLM context overflow."""
    if len(text) <= max_chars:
        return text
    lines = text.splitlines()
    kept, seen = [], set()
    for i, line in enumerate(lines):
        if SIGNAL_PATTERN.search(line):
            for j in range(max(0, i - 1), min(len(lines), i + 3)):
                if j not in seen:
                    seen.add(j)
                    kept.append((j, lines[j]))
    kept.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    signal_text = "\n".join(l for _, l in kept)
    if signal_text and len(signal_text) <= max_chars:
        return f"[Note: file truncated for length]\n\n{signal_text}"
    half = max_chars // 2
    return text[:half] + f"\n\n... [truncated] ...\n\n" + text[-half:]


def chunk_text(text: str, chunk_size: int = 600, overlap: int = 100) -> list[str]:
    chunks, start = [], 0
    while start < len(text):
        chunks.append(text[start:start + chunk_size].strip())
        start += chunk_size - overlap
    return [c for c in chunks if len(c) > 40]


# ======================================================================
# DOCUMENT <-> VECTOR HELPERS
# ======================================================================
def like_escape(s: str) -> str:
    """Escape LIKE wildcards so filenames like 'RB-001_x.md' match only themselves."""
    return s.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("%", "\\%").replace("_", "\\_")


def doc_like_pattern(filename: str) -> str:
    return f"[{like_escape(filename)}]%"


def delete_vectors(filename: str):
    supabase.table("sre_runbooks").delete().like("content", doc_like_pattern(filename)).execute()


# ======================================================================
# LLM HELPERS
# ======================================================================
def call_llm(messages: list, timeout: int = 180) -> str:
    out = ""
    tokens_in = usage.count_messages(messages)
    with llm_slot("ingest", wait_timeout=None) as waited:
        t0 = time.time()
        with requests.post(COLAB_API_URL, json={"messages": messages}, stream=True, timeout=timeout) as r:
            for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=512, decode_unicode=True):
                if chunk:
                    out += chunk
        seconds = time.time() - t0
    usage.log_call("ingest", None, tokens_in, usage.count_tokens(out), seconds, waited)
    return out


def _clean(s: str) -> str:
    s = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", s.strip().strip("\"'`")).strip()
    return s if 0 < len(s) <= MAX_FIELD_LEN else ""


def parse_triples(raw: str) -> list[tuple]:
    """Parses the LLM output defensively. Anything malformed is dropped."""
    m = re.search(r"\[.*\]", raw, re.DOTALL)
    if not m:
        return []
    try:
        data = json.loads(m.group(0))
    except Exception:
        return []
    out = []
    for t in data if isinstance(data, list) else []:
        if isinstance(t, (list, tuple)) and len(t) == 3 and all(isinstance(x, str) for x in t):
            s, r, d = _clean(t[0]), _clean(t[1]).lower(), _clean(t[2])
            if s and r and d and s.lower() != d.lower():
                out.append((s, r, d))
    return out


# ======================================================================
# STRUCTURED (DETERMINISTIC) EXTRACTION FROM FRONT MATTER
# ======================================================================
def split_front_matter(text: str):
    m = re.match(r"^---\s*\n(.*?)\n---\s*\n", text, re.DOTALL)
    if not m:
        return {}, text
    meta = {}
    for line in m.group(1).splitlines():
        k, _, v = line.partition(":")
        v = v.strip()
        if v.startswith("[") and v.endswith("]"):
            meta[k.strip()] = [x.strip() for x in v[1:-1].split(",") if x.strip()]
        else:
            meta[k.strip()] = v
    return meta, text[m.end():]


def structured_triples(meta: dict) -> list[tuple]:
    doc_id = meta.get("id")
    if not doc_id:
        return []
    dtype = meta.get("type", "")
    out = []
    title = meta.get("title")
    if title:
        rel = {"runbook": "is a runbook for", "sop": "defines",
               "incident_triage": "is an incident report about"}.get(dtype, "covers")
        out.append((doc_id, rel, title))
    for svc in meta.get("services", []):
        out.append((doc_id, "involves", svc))
    for alert in meta.get("alerts", []):
        out.append((alert, "is handled by" if dtype == "runbook" else "fired during", doc_id))
    for ref in meta.get("related", []):
        out.append((doc_id, "is related to", ref))
    return out


# ======================================================================
# GRAPH INGEST / SAFE REMOVAL (with per-document provenance)
# ======================================================================
# Every edge created from a document stores the filename in r.docs (a list).
#   - Edges with NO r.docs (seed CSV, manual additions) are PROTECTED: a document
#     upload/delete never modifies or removes them.
#   - An edge shared by several documents keeps living until its LAST document is deleted.
#   - Entity nodes are only removed if they were touched by the deleted document AND have
#     no relationships left. Nothing outside that set is ever touched.
UPSERT_EDGES = """
UNWIND $rows AS row
MERGE (a:Entity {name: row.src})
MERGE (b:Entity {name: row.dst})
MERGE (a)-[r:RELATED_TO {type: row.rel}]->(b)
ON CREATE SET r.docs = [$doc]
ON MATCH SET r.docs = CASE
    WHEN r.docs IS NULL THEN NULL
    WHEN $doc IN r.docs THEN r.docs
    ELSE r.docs + $doc END
"""


def extract_all_triples(filename: str, text: str):
    meta, body = split_front_matter(text)
    triples = structured_triples(meta)
    llm_error = None
    try:
        limit = EXTRACT_WINDOW_CHARS * EXTRACT_MAX_WINDOWS
        for i in range(0, min(len(body), limit), EXTRACT_WINDOW_CHARS):
            window = body[i:i + EXTRACT_WINDOW_CHARS]
            if len(window.strip()) < 80:
                continue
            raw = call_llm([
                {"role": "system", "content": EXTRACT_PROMPT},
                {"role": "user", "content": window},
            ])
            triples += parse_triples(raw)
    except Exception as e:
        llm_error = str(e)

    # De-duplicate (case-insensitive) and cap
    seen, unique = set(), []
    for s, r, d in triples:
        key = (s.lower(), r.lower(), d.lower())
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            unique.append((s, r, d))
    return unique[:MAX_TRIPLES_PER_DOC], llm_error


def ingest_graph(filename: str, text: str) -> dict:
    triples, llm_error = extract_all_triples(filename, text)
    if not triples:
        return {"edges": 0, "llm_error": llm_error}

    with neo4j_driver.session() as session:
        # Reuse existing entity names (case-insensitive) so we don't create duplicates
        index = {rec["name"].lower(): rec["name"]
                 for rec in session.run("MATCH (e:Entity) RETURN e.name AS name")}
        rows = []
        for s, r, d in triples:
            s = index.setdefault(s.lower(), s)
            d = index.setdefault(d.lower(), d)
            rows.append({"src": s, "rel": r, "dst": d})
        session.run(UPSERT_EDGES, rows=rows, doc=filename)
    return {"edges": len(rows), "llm_error": llm_error}


def remove_doc_graph(filename: str) -> dict:
    """Removes only what belongs exclusively to this document."""
    def _tx(tx):
        touched = tx.run(
            "MATCH (a:Entity)-[r:RELATED_TO]->(b:Entity) WHERE $doc IN r.docs "
            "RETURN a.name AS a, b.name AS b, size(r.docs) AS owners",
            doc=filename).data()
        if not touched:
            return {"edges_removed": 0, "edges_kept_shared": 0, "nodes_removed": 0}

        names = list({x for row in touched for x in (row["a"], row["b"])})
        removed = sum(1 for row in touched if row["owners"] == 1)
        shared = len(touched) - removed

        # Detach this document from every edge it owns; delete edges nobody else owns
        tx.run(
            "MATCH ()-[r:RELATED_TO]->() WHERE $doc IN r.docs "
            "WITH r, [d IN r.docs WHERE d <> $doc] AS remaining "
            "SET r.docs = remaining "
            "WITH r, remaining WHERE size(remaining) = 0 "
            "DELETE r",
            doc=filename)

        # Delete only touched entities that now have no relationships at all
        res = tx.run(
            "MATCH (e:Entity) WHERE e.name IN $names AND NOT (e)--() "
            "WITH e, e.name AS nm DELETE e RETURN count(nm) AS c",
            names=names).single()
        return {"edges_removed": removed, "edges_kept_shared": shared,
                "nodes_removed": res["c"] if res else 0}

    with neo4j_driver.session() as session:
        return session.execute_write(_tx)

@app.get("/admin", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def serve_admin_ui():
    with open(BASE_DIR / "static" / "admin.html", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return f.read()
